- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).

# Wild Hog Shard

[Wild Hog Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])

Families: None

Category: Forest
[Attribute]

Requirement: Hunting X (10) ([[Hunting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: WILD_HOG
Tooltip: Animal Fortune I Grants +0.5  Magic Find against  Animal mobs.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE FOREST SHARD (ID R46)
The Wild Hog Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Animal Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Wild Hog Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Groundhog Shard]]
- Second: [[Honeyhog Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Wild Hog Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Animal Fortune]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Animal Fortune ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Magic Find]] against [[Animal]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Wild Hog Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Wild Hog Shard has the following description:
- Contrary to popular belief, these creatures are highly intelligent.

# Winding Ivy
Categories: Rare items, Items, Pet items
[Winding Ivy]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WINDING_IVY
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Grants +10 Sweep.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; RARE PET ITEM
A Winding Ivy is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that grants +10 [[Sweep]]. It can be purchased from [[Miria]] at after reaching [[Miria|Miria's Milestone IV (4)]].
## Obtaining
### Miria
Upon reaching [[Miria|Miria's Milestone IV (4)]], the player can buy a Winding Ivy from [[Miria]] for 250 (3 stacks plus 58) [[Miria's Coupon]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Winding Ivy will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +10 [[Sweep]]. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Winding Ivy for 100,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 10, 2026|Added Winding Ivy.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Added Winding Ivy.|

# Winter Homestead Barn Skin
Categories: Rare items, Barn Skins, Seasonal Bundles
[Winter Homestead Barn Skin]
Type: Barn Skin ([[Barn Skins]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WINTER_HOMESTEAD_BARN_SKIN
Tooltip: Consume this item to unlock the Winter Homestead Barn Skin on The Garden!; Click to consume!; RARE COSMETIC
The Winter Homestead Barn Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Barn Skins|Barn Skin]] that could be obtained from a past [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Winter Homestead Barn Skin could be purchased from the Winter 2023 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.
## Usage
The Winter Homestead Barn Skin can be consumed on the [[The Garden|Garden]] and then used at [[The Garden|The Desk]] to change the look of the center plot.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Added Winter Homestead Barn Skin.|

Tooltip: Holds items commonly found on Jerry's Workshop! Items you pickup go directly into your sacks.; Capacity: 20,160 per item Multiple sacks sum their capacity!; Right Click to open sack!; RARE SACK
A Winter Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] that holds 20,160x of [[Jerry's Workshop]] related items.
## Obtaining
### Sherry
A Winter Sack can be bought from [[Sherry]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]] for 250,000 [[Coins]].
### Gift
A Winter Sack can be obtained by opening [[Red Gift|Red Gifts]]. There is a 1.81% (1 in 55.38 gifts) chance of receiving a Winter Sack from [[Red Gift|Red Gifts]].
## Usage
The Winter Sack can store items obtained on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
|Sack|Capacity|Items|
|-|-|-|
|[[Winter Sack]] Winter Sack|20,160|[[White Gift]], [[Green Gift]], [[Red Gift]], [[Party Gift]], [[Snow Block]], [[Enchanted Snow Block]], [[Snowball]], [[Ice]], [[Packed Ice]], [[Enchanted Ice]], [[Enchanted Packed Ice]], [[Hunk of Ice]], [[Hunk of Blue Ice]], [[Glacial Fragment]], [[Walnut]], [[Winter Fragment]], [[Volcanic Rock]]|
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Events Sack]]|None|1x [[Spooky Sack]] [[Winter Sack]] 1x Winter Sack 70x (1 stack plus 6 (70 in total)) [[Carnival Ticket]]|Recipe: [[Carnival Ticket]] x10 ×7 + Winter Sack/[[Spooky Sack]] + [[Spooky Sack]]/Winter Sack → [[Events Sack]]|
## Trivia
- The ID (LARGE_WINTER_SACK) implies that there could have been other sizes of the Winter Sack earlier in development.

Bazaar Material cost: (not including: 2 [[Bluetooth Ring]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: UPGRADE_STONE_SUBZERO
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Upgrades a Droplet Wisp pet to a Frost Wisp pet, resetting its level.; Right-click on your wisp to upgrade it!; RARE
Wisp Upgrade Stones are [[Rarity|RARE]]-[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] items used to upgrade the rarity of the [[Wisp Pet]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) Wisp Upgrade Stone|Blaze Slayer III (3) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Kelvin Inverter]] 1x [[Inferno Vertex]] 3x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 8x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x2 ×4 + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] ×3 + [[Inferno Vertex]] + [[Kelvin Inverter]] → Wisp Upgrade Stone|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) Wisp Upgrade Stone|Blaze Slayer V (5) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|6x [[Kelvin Inverter]] 8x [[Inferno Vertex]] 12x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 48x [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Inferno Vertex]] x2 ×4 + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x6 ×2 + [[Molten Powder]] x24 ×2 + [[Kelvin Inverter]] x6 → Wisp Upgrade Stone|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Wisp Upgrade Stone|Blaze Slayer VII (7) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|1x [[Subzero Inverter]] 1x [[Inferno Apex]] 2x [[Bluetooth Ring]] 16x [[Inferno Vertex]] 64x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Molten Powder]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] x64 ×2 + [[Inferno Apex]] + [[Bluetooth Ring]] ×2 + [[Inferno Vertex]] x8 ×2 + [[Subzero Inverter]] + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x64 → Wisp Upgrade Stone|
## Usage
A Wisp Upgrade Stone of the correct rarity can be used to upgrade a [[Wisp Pet]] to the next rarity, resetting its level to 1.
The pet must be level 80 or higher to be upgraded. It is recommended to stop leveling the pet up when it reaches level 80, unless if it is a [[Wisp Pet|Subzero Wisp Pet]].
## Trivia
- Upgrading a [[Wisp Pet|Droplet Wisp Pet]] to a certain rarity requires the following materials:
  - [[Rarity|RARE]]: 3 [[Enchanted Packed Ice]], 1 [[Kelvin Inverter|Kelvin Inverters]], 1 [[Inferno Vertex|Inferno Vertices]] and 8 [[Molten Powder]].
  - [[Rarity|EPIC]]: 15 [[Enchanted Packed Ice]], 7 [[Kelvin Inverter|Kelvin Inverters]], 9 [[Inferno Vertex|Inferno Vertices]] and 56 [[Molten Powder]].
  - [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]]: 79 (1 stack plus 15) [[Enchanted Packed Ice]], 7 [[Kelvin Inverter|Kelvin Inverters]], 25 [[Inferno Vertex|Inferno Vertices]], 184 (2 stacks plus 56) [[Molten Powder]], 2 [[Bluetooth Ring|Bluetooth Rings]], 1 [[Subzero Inverter]] and 1 [[Inferno Apex]].
- Wisp Upgrade Stones can be used without meeting their craft requirements.
- Wisp Upgrade Stones cannot be [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]]. If one is already recombobulated, it will be forcibly reverted to its base rarity.
  - Previously, players would recombobulate them to scam other players, as recombobulating them made them falsely appear as a higher tier upgrade stone.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 21, 2022|Added Wisp Upgrade Stones.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Wisp Upgrade Stones.|
|Oct 4, 2023|0.19.5|Changed the recipe for all Wisp Upgrade Stones.|

# Witch Mask
Categories: Rare items, Armor, Crafting ingredients, Helmet
[Witch Mask]
Type: [[Armor]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: Top 10 in [[Spooky Festival]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 66; [[Defense]]: 111
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WITCH_MASK
Tooltip: Health: +66; Defense: +111; Ability: Bat Swarm Summons two bats that will follow you and attack nearby enemies, dealing 4,000 damage upon exploding. Your bats will not spawn on your island.; This item can be reforged!; RARE HELMET
The Witch Mask is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] that is obtainable by placing in the top 10 during a [[Spooky Festival]] or getting 2,000 points or above in the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Obtaining
The Witch Mask can be obtained by placing in the top 10 during a [[Spooky Festival]] or getting 2,000 points or above in the [[Spooky Festival]].
## Usage
Helmet:
Witch Mask [[Health]]: +66 [[Defense]]: +111
[Effects]
[Ability: Bat Swarm; Summons two bats that will follow you and attack nearby enemies, dealing 4,000 damage upon exploding. Your bats will not spawn on your island.]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Vampire Witch Mask]]|None|[[Witch Mask]] 1x Witch Mask 1x [[Vampire Mask]] 7x [[Ectoplasm]]|Recipe: [[Ectoplasm]] ×7 + Witch Mask + [[Vampire Mask]] → [[Vampire Witch Mask]]|
## Tips
- Equipping the Witch Mask with a [[Bat Person Talisman]] (or higher) will grant the player a small bonus to [[Defense]], [[Strength]], and [[Intelligence]].
## Trivia
- The Witch Mask used to allow AFK killing entities but was later patched by disabling [[Telekinesis (Enchantment)|Telekinesis]].
- The Witch Mask replaced the [[Vampire Mask]] as a [[Spooky Festival]] reward.
- The Witch Mask can regularly damage [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]] without damage reduction.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 24, 2019|0.7.3|Added Witch Mask.|
|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Buffed ability's damage from 3,000 to 4,000.|
|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Removed the [[Ability Damage]] scaling of the ability.|

The Wizard's Breadcrumbs are a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtained from [[Wizard But Weird]].
## Obtaining
The Wizard's Breadcrumbs can be purchased by speaking to the [[Wizard But Weird]] in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]].
## Usage
The Wizard's Breadcrumbs can be used to show the player yellow particles that lead back to the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]]. Fully using it and arriving at the tower will remove it from the player's inventory and allow them to unlock the [[Rift Guide]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Guaranteed Message|
|-|-|
|Not close enough to crumbs|Mmh... I should look ahead for more crumbs...|
|Finding the last crumb by the tower|The breadcrumbs disappear in your hand... I should go talk to the Wizard again...|

The Wizard's Crystal is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] item that can be found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] from [[The Catacombs - Floor IV|Floor IV]] to [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]], more precisely in the Catacombs Rooms/Wizard Room. Once found, players can give the Wizard's Crystal to the [[Wizard (Catacombs)]], located in the same room. Upon giving him the crystal, the team gains 1 [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing of Wisdom]] of level Ⅰ or Ⅱ. This does not count as a [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret]].
## Gallery
- The Wizard
- The location of the Wizard's Crystal
## Trivia
- The Wizard's Crystal used to be able to be taken out of Dungeons through the [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]], among other methods.
- When right clicking to claim the crystal, the player is told "You found a Special Crystal"

# Wolf Fur Mixin
Categories: Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Brewing, Potions, Slayer
[Wolf Fur Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Bartender]]
Buy: 150,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WOLF_FUR_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Gain 7 Magic Find when slaying monsters in one hit!; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); ☠ Requires Wolf Slayer 8.; RARE
Wolf Fur Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Purchase
A Wolf Fur Mixin can be purchased from the [[Bartender]] for 150,000 [[Coins]] once the player reaches [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer VIII (8)]].
## Usage
### Brewing
A Wolf Fur Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +7 [[Magic Find]] when slaying monsters in one hit. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Wolf Fur Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## Trivia
- The Wolf Fur Mixin's effect does not activate if the mob hits the player first, even if it is then killed in one hit.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 12, 2021|Added Wolf Fur Mixin.|
|Unknown Date|God Potion Mixin effects no longer persist across servers.|
|Mar 18, 2021|Fixed God Potion Mixin effects not persisting across servers.|
|Unknown Date|God Potion Mixin effects do not persist across servers, again.|
|Mar 29, 2021|Fixed God Potion Mixin effects not persisting across servers, again.|
Linked from: [[Magic Find]], [[Magic Find/Alpha]], [[Magic Find/Table]], [[Mixins]], [[Wolf Slayer]]

# Yellow Goblin Egg
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crystal Hollows
[Yellow Goblin Egg]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: What came first the goblin or the egg?
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOBLIN_EGG_YELLOW
Tooltip: What came first the goblin or the egg?; RARE
Yellow Goblin Egg is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used to forge the [[Sunny Side Goblin Omelette]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv89]|[[Creeperlobber]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
|[Lv65]|[[Goblin Flamethrower]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
|[Lv96]|[[Murderlover]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
### Treasure Chests
Yellow Goblin Eggs can be dropped from chests in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. The chests can either be found in naturally spawning structures like the [[Goblin Queen's Den]] and by [[King Yolkar]] or can be spawned by the player while mining [[Hard Stone]] in the [[Goblin Holdout]].
### [[Frozen Corpses]]
Goblin Eggs can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis|[[Yellow Goblin Egg]] 1 Yellow Goblin Egg|4.18% (1 in 23.93)|
|Lapis|[[Yellow Goblin Egg]] 2 Yellow Goblin Egg|2.09% (1 in 47.86)|
|Lapis|[[Yellow Goblin Egg]] 4 Yellow Goblin Egg|1.04% (1 in 95.71)|
## Usage
### Spawning Golden Goblins
Throwing a Yellow Goblin Egg in [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]] or [[Glacite Tunnels]] has a 3.33% (1 in 30 eggs) chance to spawn a [[Golden Goblin]], with a base 3.33% chance to be replaced with [[Diamond Goblin]].
### Forging
#### [[Sunny Side Goblin Omelette]]
- 1 [[Sunny Side Goblin Omelette]]
  - [[Yellow Goblin Egg]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) Yellow Goblin Egg
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Topaz Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Topaz Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Topaz Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Topaz Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
### Fuel
The Yellow Goblin Egg can be used as a [[Machine Fuel]], adding 10,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]].

# Zombie Brain Mixin
Categories: Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Brewing, Potions, Slayer
[Zombie Brain Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Bartender]]
Buy: 150,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ZOMBIE_BRAIN_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Gain +10 Ferocity!; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); ☠ Requires Zombie Slayer 8.; RARE
Zombie Brain Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Purchase
A Zombie Brain Mixin can be purchased from the [[Bartender]] for 150,000 [[Coins]] once the player reaches [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer VIII (8)]].
## Usage
### Brewing
A Zombie Brain Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +10 [[Ferocity]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Zombie Brain Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 12, 2021|Added Zombie Brain Mixin.|
|Unknown Date|God Potion Mixin effects no longer persist across servers.|
|Mar 18, 2021|Fixed God Potion Mixin effects not persisting across servers.|
|Unknown Date|God Potion Mixin effects do not persist across servers, again.|
|Mar 29, 2021|Fixed God Potion Mixin effects not persisting across servers, again.|

## Trivia
- The [[Achievements|"It Never Ends" Achievement]] can be earned by killing a [[Zombie]] with the [[Zombie Sword]] Zombie Sword, [[Ornate Zombie Sword]], or [[Florid Zombie Sword]].
- The Zombie Sword is one of several direct references to [[References to other Hypixel minigames|Mega Walls]], another Hypixel game.
  - In Mega Walls, the Zombie class has a right-click ability which heals itself for a certain amount of health (and its teammates to a lesser extent), hence the Zombie Sword's similar functionality.

Shard Item ID: ZOMBUDDY
Tooltip: Mutation Serendipity I Mutations have a +0.1% chance to instantly re-mutate at their first growth phase when harvested.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE COMBAT SHARD (ID R84)
The Zombuddy Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Mutation Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Zombuddy Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Zombuddy]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Zombuddy Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Zombuddy Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mutation Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mutation Serendipity ([[Attributes]]); Mutations have a +0.1%–1% chance to instantly re-mutate at their first growth phase when harvested.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Zombuddy Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Zombuddy Shard has the following description:
- Just wants to give you a warm hug for the rest of eternity.

# Reforge Stones
Aliases: Reforge Stone, Reforge stone, Reforge stones
Categories: Reforge stones, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Pages with Missing Recipes, Reforging, Dungeons
Reforge Stones are items that can be used to apply a specific [[Reforging|reforge]] to compatible items, including [[Armor]], [[Swords]], [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]], [[Bows]], [[Farming Tools]], [[Axes]], [[Fishing Rods]], [[Equipment]], [[Fishing Nets]], and [[Vacuums]].
## Description
Reforge Stones grant special reforges that cannot be obtained through [[Reforging|basic reforging]]. An item can only have one reforge at a time, and applying another reforge replaces the existing one.
## How to Use
Reforge Stones can be applied through an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]]. When using an Anvil, place the Reforge Stone on the right side of the anvil and the item that the player wants to reforge on the left.
The [[Coins]] cost for applying a Reforge Stone depends on the rarity of the Reforge Stone, rather than the rarity of the item being reforged. The costs range from 5,000 [[Coins]] for [[Rarity|COMMON]] Reforge Stones to 2,000,000 [[Coins]] for [[Rarity|DIVINE]] Reforge Stones.
Reforge Stones can be right-clicked to view their stats at all rarities and their reforge bonus. The [[Blacksmith|Reforge Guide]] can be accessed with /reforges, through [[The Hex]], or through the [[Blacksmith]]'s menu.

## Trivia
- The three dragon-related Reforge Stones (Scale, Claw, and Horn) are most likely references to the Nintendo game Breath of the Wild released in 2017, in which players can shoot the three dragons Dinraal, Naydra, and Farosh for item materials. When the Scale, Claw, or Horn is targeted, the player will receive the corresponding item. Just like in the Breath of the Wild, the [[Dragon Scale]] is the worst, followed by the [[Dragon Claw]] and the [[Dragon Horn]] as the best.
- Most Reforge Stones have [[Mining]] requirements, but some use a different Skill or have no Skill requirement. For example, [[Salt Cube]] and [[Rusty Anchor]] require [[Fishing]], while [[Deep Sea Orb]] and [[Calcified Heart]] have no Skill requirement.

# Agaricus Cap
Categories: Rift Dimension, Common items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Agaricus Cap Collection, Collections, Farming
Rift Exclusive Agaricus Cap is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Agaricus Cap]
Type: Item
Rarity: COMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Agaricus Cap Bunch]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: AGARICUS_CAP
Tooltip: Collection Item; COMMON
Agaricus Cap is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] Collection item obtained from [[Dreadfarm]].
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|20|[[Agaricus Cap Bunch]] Recipe|+4|
|II|60|[[Agaricus Soup]] Recipe|+4|
|III|100|[[Agaricus Cap Cap]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|200|[[Agaricus Chumcap]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Agaricus Cap can be harvested with a [[Wand of Farming]] in the [[Dreadfarm]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. The player must stare at the [[Mushroom|Brown Mushrooms]] on the ground for 3 to 5 seconds until they briefly turn into [[Mushroom|Red Mushrooms]], at which point they can be collected.
Tip: Keeping the mouse button held down during the collection phase and basically 'mining' the Agaricus Cap, keeps the server from 'rejecting' the click and therefore speeds up the process of collecting these.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Agaricus Cap Cap]]|Agaricus Cap III (3)|1x [[Not-Dead-Gehog Mask]] [[Agaricus Cap]] 50x Agaricus Cap|Recipe: Agaricus Cap x10 ×5 + [[Not-Dead-Gehog Mask]] → [[Agaricus Cap Cap]]|
|[[Agaricus Soup]]|Agaricus Cap II (2)|1x [[Plumber's Bucket]] [[Agaricus Cap]] 4x Agaricus Cap|Recipe: Agaricus Cap x2 ×2 + [[Plumber's Bucket]] → [[Agaricus Soup]]|
|[[Agaricus Cap Bunch]]|Agaricus Cap I (1)|[[Agaricus Cap]] 20x Agaricus Cap|Recipe: Agaricus Cap x4 ×7 → [[Agaricus Cap Bunch]]|
|[[Agaricus Chumcap]]|Agaricus Cap IV (4)|1x [[Plumber's Bucket]] [[Agaricus Cap]] 8x Agaricus Cap|Recipe: Agaricus Cap ×8 + [[Plumber's Bucket]] → [[Agaricus Chumcap]] x8|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Agaricus Cap.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Agaricus Cap.|

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Agaricus Cap IV (4) ([[Agaricus Cap]])|1x [[Plumber's Bucket]] 8x [[Agaricus Cap]]|Recipe: [[Agaricus Cap]] ×8 + [[Plumber's Bucket]] → Agaricus Chumcap x8|
## Usage
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Agaricus Chumcap can also be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], Agaricus Chumcap can be used as a [[Crafting|crafting ingredient]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ANTI_MORPH_POTION
Tooltip: Right-click to throw the potion at ugly people.; EPIC
The Anti-Morph Potion is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
An Anti-Morph Potion can be obtained from crafting it in the [[Alchemist (Rift)|Alchemist]]'s cauldron in the [[Rift Dimension]] with 8 [[Larva Silk]], 64 (1 stack) [[Riftwart Roots]] and 4 [[Caducous Extract]]. Subsequent Anti-Morph Potions can be purchased from [[Alchemist (Rift)|Alchemist]] for 1,000 [[Motes]] each.
## Usage
The player can splash an Anti-Morph Potion on [[Detransfigured Seraphine]] to turn her into [[Detransfigured Seraphine|Transfigured Seraphine]], unlocking her shop.

# Around Colosseum
Categories: Rift Dimension, Locations
Rift Exclusive Around Colosseum is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Around Colosseum]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[West Village]]
Coordinates: -186 71 -39
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Detective Amog]] [[Master Tactician Fink]]
[Metadata]
ID: RIFT_COLOSSEUM_AROUND
Around Colosseum is a [[Locations|Location]] within the [[West Village]] and around the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
Around Colosseum serves as a way for players to walk around the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]]. It contains a launch pad, which allows players to access [[Mole (NPC)|Mole]] above the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]], as well as [[Detective Amog]] and the [[Master Tactician Fink]] during the reverse-murder quest.
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in Around Colosseum:

|-|-|-|-|-|

|[[Detective Amog]]|[[Detective Amog]]|Around Colosseum; -188, 71, -56.718 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during his second reverse-murder quest.|✕|
|[[Master Tactician Fink]]|[[Master Tactician Fink]]|Around Colosseum; -191.93, 72, -57 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells the [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]] during [[Detective Amog]]'s first reverse-murder quest.|✔|

- A total of 32 Bloodbadges are required to craft all the non [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]] items, requiring 64x [[Cruxmotion]], [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] and [[Bacte Fragment]] as well as 2048 [[Coven Seal|Coven Seals]].
  - For [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]] items ( [[Vampire Dentist Relic]] and [[Weirder Tuba]]) for both inside and outside the rift without buying items from the Auction House, additional 9 (41 in total) Bloodbadges are required.
  - Players can save Bloodbadges and still receive all the items by buying [[Vampire Dentist Relic]] and [[Weirder Tuba]] from the Auction House.
  - 64x [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] and [[Bacte Fragment]] requires killing [[Leech Supreme]] and [[Bacte]] a total of 8 times each.

Bottled Odonata is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is used to craft the [[Supreme Timecharm]].
## Obtaining
A Bottled Odonata can be obtained looking at an [[Odonata]] while holding an [[Empty Odonata Bottle]] and being within a 3 block radius of it. The player must look at the Odonata for another 3 seconds to successfully catch it and transform the Empty Odonata Bottle into a Bottled Odonata.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Supreme Timecharm]]|None|4x [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] [[Bottled Odonata]] 4x Bottled Odonata 24x [[Deadgehog Spine]] 32x [[Lil' Pad]]|Recipe: Bottled Odonata ×4 + [[Lil' Pad]] x16 ×2 + [[Deadgehog Spine]] x12 ×2 + [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] x4 → [[Supreme Timecharm]]|
### Merchants
[[Bottled Odonata]] 1 Bottled Odonata and 200 [[Motes]] can be used to buy a [[Harmonious Surgery Toolkit]] from the [[Argofay Bugshopper]] after buying a [[Larva Hook]].

|-|-|-|
|Caducous Stem III (3) ([[Caducous Stem]])|3x [[Plumber's Bucket]] 20x [[Caducous Stem]]|Recipe: [[Plumber's Bucket]] ×3 + [[Caducous Stem]] x20 → Caducous Extract|
## Usage
The player can give the [[Alchemist (Rift)|Alchemist]] [[Caducous Extract]] 4 Caducous Extract, 8 [[Larva Silk]], 64 (1 stack) [[Riftwart Roots]] and a [[Plumber's Bucket]] to obtain an [[Anti-Morph Potion]].

# Caducous Feeder
Aliases: Feeder
Categories: Rift Dimension, Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Caducous Stem Collection, Tools, Other Tools
Rift Exclusive Caducous Feeder is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Caducous Feeder]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 448x (7 stacks (448 items total)) [[Caducous Stem]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CADUCOUS_FEEDER
Tooltip: Use on a pet to consume an Ultimate Carrot Candy and grant it 1,650,000; pet Exp.; Has 2 charges, reloading after 20h.; ❣ Requires Profile older than 7 days.; X Rift-Transferable X RARE
Caducous Feeder is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Caducous Stem IV (4) ([[Caducous Stem]])|448x [[Caducous Stem]]|Recipe: [[Caducous Stem]] x64 ×7 → Caducous Feeder|
## Usage
Caducous Feeder can be used on any [[Pets|Pet]] to consume an [[Ultimate Carrot Candy]] and instead grant it 1,650,000 pet experience. The item has 2 charges, and reloads after 20 hours. Carrot Candies fed in this way don't count toward the candy limit. It cannot be used on pets that are max level.
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Caducous Feeder can also be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], the Caducous Feeder can be used as intended.
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|On cooldown|You may only use this item 2 times per 20 hours!|
|Profile not old enough|You do not have the requirements to use this item! You need Profile older than 7 days to use it!|
|Feeding a max level pet|This pet doesn't want candies! They are already full enough!|
|Feeding a Montezuma ([[Montezuma Pet]]) or a Gabagool ([[Gabagool (disambiguation)]]) pet|This pet doesn't want candies! In fact it's quite offended at the proposition.|
|Trying to feed a pet without having Ultimate Carrot Candy|Using the Cadacous Feeder requires consuming an Ultimate Carrot Candy! You do not have any Ultimate Carrot Candy in your inventory!|
## Trivia
- The player's profile needs to be older than 7 days to be able to use the Caducous Feeder.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Caducous Feeder.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Caducous Feeder.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Added a confirmation menu to the Caducous Feeder.|
|Jan 16, 2024|0.19.10|Caducous Feeder now grants 1,650,000 XP instead of 1,100,000.|

# Deadgehog
Aliases: Dead Hedgehog
Categories: Rift Dimension, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Rift Exclusive Deadgehog is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Deadgehog]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 2
Spawn Location: [[Black Lagoon]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 150; [[Rift Damage]]: 9
[Drops]
Drops: 3–4x [[Deadgehog Spine]]
XP: +6 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: DEADGEHOG_2
The Deadgehog is a mob that spawns in the [[Black Lagoon]]. It has many arrows sticking out of its body.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv2]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Deadgehog Spine]]|3|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Deadgehog Spine]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv2]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|150|150|150|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|6|6|
## Behavior
Deadgehogs attack nearby players by charging up and shooting a volley of arrows at the player, each of which reducing their [[Rift Time]] by 9 seconds. Players can avoid these arrows by hiding behind the Mushrooms that appear in the Lagoon.

[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Deadgehog|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Deadgehog.|

### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|

# Discrite
Aliases: Spotlite
Categories: Rift Dimension, Rare items, Legendary items, Items, Craftable items, Timite Collection, Aging Items
Rift Exclusive Discrite is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Discrite]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Timite IV (4) ([[Timite]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DISCRITE
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable: ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum: ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPOTLITE
Tooltip: Consume to boost your  Health and  Rift Time for 60m.; Keep this item in your inventory to increase the bonus up to +150 Health and +150 Rift Time, at which point the item evolves!; Current Bonus: +0 Rift Time; PRO TIP!;  Health is an overworld stat- but you'll still be granted it when you go back!; X Rift-Exportable X Loses motes value when crossing dimensions.; RARE
Discrite is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that grants [[Rift Time]] and [[Health]]. After reaching 150 bonus [[Rift Time]] and [[Health]], it evolves into Spotlite.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Timite IV (4) ([[Timite]])|64x [[Youngite]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Timite]] 256x [[Obsolite]]|Recipe: [[Obsolite]] x64 ×4 + [[Timite]] x64 ×4 + [[Youngite]] x64 → Discrite|
## Usage
Upon reaching 150 bonus [[Rift Time]] and [[Health]], Discrite evolves into Spotlite with the following message:
EVOLUTION! Your Discrite has evolved!
Consuming Spotlite grants 150 [[Rift Time]] and 150 [[Health]], for 60 minutes. It also grants a permanent 25 [[Rift Time]] and 5 [[Health]]. The permanent buff can only be applied 5 times.
It gains 0.5 [[Rift Time]] and 0.5 [[Health]] every hour, taking 300 hours to reach the maximum buff of 150 bonus [[Rift Time]] and [[Health]]. This time can be sped up 2x by placing Discrite into [[Time Pocket]]. Furthermore, the [[Enchanted Clock]] and the Quiz Puzzle in the [[Catacombs]] increases the timer on all [[Time Pocket|Aging Items]] by 1 hour, which is doubled for items placed in the [[Time Pocket]].
One Discrite is also used to upgrade the [[Time Pocket]] from 6 slots to 9 slots and one Spotlite is used to upgrade it from 9 slots to 12 slots.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 10, 2026|Changed the [[Health Regen]] bonus to instead grant [[Health]].|
|Jun 10, 2026|Nerfed the required stat buffs for conversion on Discrite from 300 to 150, but kept the bonuses at 300 on Spotlite.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Discrite.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed the [[Health Regen]] bonus to instead grant [[Health]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Nerfed the required stat buffs for conversion on Discrite from 300 to 150, but kept the bonuses at 300 on Spotlite.|
|Aug 6, 2026|Fixed Discrite not evolving into Spotlite when reaching maximum time (150 hours)|

# Empty Odonata Bottle
Categories: Rift Dimension, Uncommon items, Items
Rift Exclusive Empty Odonata Bottle is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Empty Odonata Bottle]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EMPTY_ODONATA_BOTTLE
Tooltip: Hold this and look at an Odonata for 3s while very close to bottle it.; UNCOMMON
The Empty Odonata Bottle is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Empty Odonata Bottle is unlocked in the [[Argofay Bugshopper]]'s shop once the player has purchased a [[Larva Hook]] from them.
Once unlocked, an Empty Odonata Bottle can be purchased for 2 [[Shy Crux]] and 400 [[Motes]] from the [[NPC]].
## Usage
An Empty Odonata Bottle can be used to obtain a [[Bottled Odonata]] by holding the bottle and looking at an [[Odonata]] while within a 3 block radius of it.
The player must continue looking at the [[Odonata]] for 3 seconds to successfully catch it and transform the Empty Odonata Bottle into a [[Bottled Odonata]].
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Bottled Unsuccessfully|Oops!|
|Bottled Successfully|Bottled!|
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Empty Odonata Bottle.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: EXPORTABLE_CARROTS
Tooltip: Bring 3,000 of these to Carrolyn in Scarleton on the Crimson Isle to permanently gain +5 Health and +12 Carrot Fortune.; X Rift-Transferable X UNCOMMON
Exportable Carrots are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item used in [[Carrolyn]]'s quest. They are [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Half-Eaten Carrot III (3) ([[Half-Eaten Carrot]])|1x [[Scribe Crux]] 1x [[Nearly-Whole Carrot]] 1x [[Volt Crux]]|Recipe: [[Volt Crux]] + [[Nearly-Whole Carrot]] + [[Scribe Crux]] → Exportable Carrots x64|
|Half-Eaten Carrot III (3) ([[Half-Eaten Carrot]])|1x [[Scribe Crux]] 1x [[Volt Crux]] 120x (1 stack plus 56 (120 in total)) [[Half-Eaten Carrot]]|Recipe: [[Volt Crux]] + [[Half-Eaten Carrot]] x40 ×3 + [[Scribe Crux]] → Exportable Carrots x48|
## Usage
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Exportable Carrots can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. The player can then bring [[Exportable Carrots]] 3,000 (46 stacks plus 56) Exportable Carrots to [[Carrolyn]] in [[Scarleton]] to gain a permanent +5 [[Health]] and +12 [[Crop Fortune|Carrot Fortune]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Capsaicin Eyedrops]]|Chili Pepper III (3) ([[Chili Pepper]])|1x [[Water Hydra Head]] 4x [[Chili Pepper]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Carrot]] [[Exportable Carrots]] 128x Exportable Carrots|Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] ×4 + Exportable Carrots x64 ×2 + [[Enchanted Carrot]] x64 ×2 + [[Water Hydra Head]] → [[Capsaicin Eyedrops]] x3|
## Trivia
- To obtain [[Exportable Carrots]] 3,000 Exportable Carrots, the player will need 4,554 (71 stacks plus 10) [[Half-Eaten Carrot|Half-Eaten Carrots]], 47 [[Volt Crux]] and 47 [[Scribe Crux]].
  - If the second recipe (with [[Half-Eaten Carrot|Half-Eaten Carrots]] instead of [[Nearly-Whole Carrot|Nearly-Whole Carrots]]) is used, it will require 63 [[Volt Crux]] and 63 [[Scribe Crux]].

Type: Item

A Frozen Water Pungi is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|8x [[Nullified Metal]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Frozen Water]]|Recipe: [[Frozen Water]] x16 ×8 + [[Nullified Metal]] x8 → Frozen Water Pungi|
## Usage
Item:
Frozen Water Pungi
[Effects]
[Ability: Snake Taming HOLD RIGHT CLICK; Aim at a Living Metal Snake to deeply calm it, allowing another player to mine it.]
The Frozen Water Pungi's Snake Taming ability plays a song: aiming it at a [[Living Cave|Living Metal Snake]] will cause it to stop moving, allowing the player to break it from the tail to the head and obtain a [[Living Metal Heart]]..
## Trivia
- Despite the tooltip's encouragement to work with another player, [[Living Metal Heart|Living Metal Hearts]] can also be obtained solo by holding the Frozen Water Pungi, switching to a pickaxe, mining the tail, then switching back to the pungi before the snake can move. This process can be repeated until the head is destroyed.

# Frozille
Categories: Rift Dimension, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
Rift Exclusive Frozille is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Frozille]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 5
Spawn Location: [[Living Stillness]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 135; [[Rift Damage]]: 5
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Frosty Crux]]
XP: +6 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: FROZILLE_5
The Frozille is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Living Stillness]]. It appears as a floating head.
## Drops
[[Frosty Crux]] drops are affected by the [[Crux Talisman]] and its upgrades.
Item drops - [Lv5]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Frosty Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv5]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|150|150|150|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|6|6|
### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.
Item drops - [Lv5]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Frosty Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Behavior
Frozilles are passive until damaged by a player. After being attacked several times, they rapidly fire several Bolts at nearby players whenever they are hit or every 3 seconds. Each Bolt removes 4 [[Rift Time]] on contact.
When at very low [[Health]], a Frozille teleports a few blocks away, becomes invulnerable, and surrounds itself with a cube of Ice. If all of the Ice is not broken within 10 seconds, the Frozille uses Ice Boom, removing 180 [[Rift Time]] from nearby players. If all of the Ice is broken, the explosion is prevented and the Frozille remains alive with very low [[Health]].
## Tips
- The [[Ice]] spawned by Frozilles can be instantly broken, but using a pickaxe slows down the process, so holding another item makes it faster to break.

[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Larva Silk
Categories: Rift Dimension, Uncommon items, Items
Rift Exclusive Larva Silk is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Larva Silk]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LARVA_SILK
Tooltip: Click two ledge blocks with this item to setup a Silkwire.; May the larvas which died for it rest in pieces.; UNCOMMON
Larva Silk is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Attacking a [[Larva]] 3 times in the [[Wyld Woods]] with a [[Larva Hook]] grants Larva Silk.
## Usage
### Silkwire
Larva Silk can be applied to two blocks to create a Silkwire between them. The player can then use a [[Silkwire Stick]] to attach themselves to the wire, allowing them to cross between two branches with ease and reach [[Odonata|Odonatas]] with less difficulty.
If the player stays on the wire for too long, it will collapse, dropping the player. It will refuse to generate if the angle of the wire is too steep or the player attempts to place a string on a slab.
### Quest
The player must bring the [[Argofay Bughunter]] [[Larva Silk]] 1 Larva Silk after they have presented the Bughunter with a [[Larva Hook]]. The player will then need to bring them another [[Larva Silk]] 6 Larva Silk to progress in the quest and claim a [[Silkwire Stick]].
Additionally, giving [[Larva Silk]] 16 Larva Silk to [[Reed]] will complete Step #1 and grant the player a permanent +20 [[Rift Time]].
Giving [[Larva Silk]] 8 Larva Silk to [[Alchemist (Rift)|Alchemist]] is part of what is required to complete the [[Detransfigured Seraphine]] quest.
## Trivia
- Assuming a separate Silkwire is created for each documented one-time objective, a total of [[Larva Silk]] 41 Larva Silk are consumed: [[Larva Silk]] 7 Larva Silk for the [[Argofay Bughunter]], [[Larva Silk]] 2 Larva Silk for the Silkwire required to capture an [[Odonata]] during their quest, [[Larva Silk]] 8 Larva Silk for four [[Enigma Souls]], [[Larva Silk]] 16 Larva Silk for [[Reed]], and [[Larva Silk]] 8 Larva Silk for [[Alchemist (Rift)|Alchemist]] as part of the [[Detransfigured Seraphine]] quest.
  - The four Enigma Souls are Between Branches, Between Roofs, Between Cooler Roofs, and Between Towers. Each requires a separate Silkwire at its documented location.
  - One [[Larva Hook]] can be used to obtain Larva Silk up to 8 times before disappearing. Without accounting for additional Silk from the [[Harmonious Surgery Toolkit]], obtaining all [[Larva Silk]] 41 Larva Silk requires 6 [[Larva Hook|Larva Hooks]].
  - Additional Larva Silk used to create optional Silkwires is not included in the total above.
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Living Metal
Categories: Rift Dimension, Rare items, Items, Crafting ingredients
Rift Exclusive Living Metal is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[Living Metal]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 5 [[Motes]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LIVING_METAL
Tooltip: It snakes around your blocky hand as you hold it.; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
Living Metal is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtained from the [[Living Cave]] in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].
## Obtaining
Living Metal can be obtained by mining the Lapis Ore in the [[Living Cave]] using a [[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]]. After 1 block is mined, another will appear adjacent to the mined block. If the player continues to mine blocks, they will gain a multiplier on the amount of Living Metal they receive. Every 10 blocks mined increases the multiplier by 1, starting from 1x, up to 5x. 3 seconds after the player stops mining blocks, or when they reach 5x multiplier, it will be reset and the player will receive the Living Metal they mined. The player will receive 20x Living Metal at a 5x multiplier.
Living Metal received in the [[Living Stillness]] is doubled.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Living Timecharm]]|None|20x [[Frosty Crux]] [[Living Metal]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Living Metal|Recipe: Living Metal x20 ×8 + [[Frosty Crux]] x20 → [[Living Timecharm]]|
|[[Living Metal Anchor]]|Living Metal Heart II (2) ([[Living Metal Heart]])|9x [[Living Metal Heart]] [[Living Metal]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Living Metal|Recipe: [[Living Metal Heart]] x3 ×3 + Living Metal x32 ×4 → [[Living Metal Anchor]]|
|[[Living Metal Bootspawn]]|None|[[Living Metal]] 4x Living Metal|Recipe: Living Metal ×4 → [[Living Metal Bootspawn]]|
|[[Living Metal Capspawn]]|None|[[Living Metal]] 5x Living Metal|Recipe: Living Metal ×5 → [[Living Metal Capspawn]]|
|[[Living Metal Chestspawn]]|None|[[Living Metal]] 8x Living Metal|Recipe: Living Metal ×8 → [[Living Metal Chestspawn]]|
|[[Living Metal Pantspawn]]|None|[[Living Metal]] 7x Living Metal|Recipe: Living Metal ×7 → [[Living Metal Pantspawn]]|
|[[Nothing-in-a-Bottle]]|Living Metal Heart III (3) ([[Living Metal Heart]])|1x [[Living Metal Heart]] [[Living Metal]] 8x Living Metal|Recipe: Living Metal ×8 + [[Living Metal Heart]] → [[Nothing-in-a-Bottle]]|

The player can secure the Living Timecharm in the [[Rift Gallery]] to gain [[SkyBlock Levels|+100 SkyBlock XP]] and unlock access to the [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]].

RIFTASTIC! [player] has secured Living Timecharm into their gallery! gg!
### Rift Necklace
Securing the Living Timecharm will add additional [[Stats]] to the [[Rift Necklace]] both inside and outside the [[Rift Dimension]].

# MrahcemiT esrevrorriM
Categories: Rift Dimension, Special items, Items
Rift Exclusive MrahcemiT esrevrorriM is only obtainable within [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
[mrahcemiT esrevrorriM]
Type: Rift Timecharm ([[Rift Timecharms]])
Rarity: SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RIFT_TROPHY_MIRRORED
Tooltip: Place this timecharm in the Rift Gallery to secure it forever!; * Soulbound * SPECIAL RIFT TIMECHARM
The mrahcemiT esrevrorriM (Mirrorverse Timecharm) is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Rift Timecharms|Rift Timecharm]].
## Obtaining
The player can purchase a mrahcemiT esrevrorriM from [[Dr. Emmett]] after completing all seven [[Mirrorverse]] puzzles for 10 [[Shadow Crux|Shadow Cruxes]] 10,000 [[Motes]].
## Usage
The player can secure the mrahcemiT esrevrorriM in the [[Rift Gallery]] to gain [[SkyBlock Levels|+100 SkyBlock XP]], and along with the [[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]], to unlock access to the [[Village Plaza]] and thus all of the following locations:
- [[Barry Center]]
- [[Barry HQ]]
- [[Empty Bank]]
- [[Book in a Book]]
- [[Déjà Vu Alley]]
- [[Half-Eaten Cave]]
- [[Lonely Terrace]]
- [[Murder House]]
- [[Taylor's]]
- [["Your" Island]]
### Game Message
Securing the mrahcemiT esrevrorriM will result in the following message, which will be broadcast to the entire lobby:
RIFTASTIC! [player] has secured mrahcemiT esrevrorriM into their gallery! gg!
### Rift Necklace
Securing the mrahcemiT esrevrorriM will add additional [[Stats]] to the [[Rift Necklace]] both inside and outside the [[Rift Dimension]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added mrahcemiT esrevrorriM.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added mrahcemiT esrevrorriM.|

- Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear is the longest item name in the game, with 55 characters (50 without spaces).

The Proto-Chicken is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item obtained in [[Dreadfarm]]. It is used to craft the [[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]].
## Obtaining
### Quest
The player can claim [[Proto-Chicken]] 1 Proto-Chicken from [[Shania (Rift)|Shania]] in [[Dreadfarm]] after giving them 2 [[Caducous Stem Bunch|Caducous Stem Bunches]], 2 [[Wilted Berberis Bunch|Wilted Berberis Bunches]] and 2 [[Agaricus Cap Bunch|Agaricus Cap Bunches]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- This item's lore and name were based off of the classic thought experiment "What came first, the chicken or the egg?" The item's lore is specifically the basis of a very popular "egg came first" argument.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SATELITE
Tooltip: High altitude, slow attitude. The procrastinator's dream stone.; Ability: Bullet Time Slow down the loss of  Rift Time by 20% in elevated areas.; LEGENDARY ACCESSORY
Satelite is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that reduces [[Rift Time]] loss in certain areas of [[Rift Dimension|The Rift]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Timite V (5) ([[Timite]])|1x [[Highlite]] 32x [[Obsolite]] 32x [[Timite]] 64x [[Youngite]]|Recipe: [[Youngite]] x16 ×4 + [[Timite]] x16 ×2 + [[Obsolite]] x16 ×2 + [[Highlite]] → Satelite|
## Usage
While held in the inventory, Satelite slows down the loss of [[Rift Time]] by 20% while the player is in [[The Mountaintop]] and its sub-locations, like the [[Continuum]] or [[Walk of Fame]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]

    - 1 [[Cruxmotion]] is kept.
    - A total of 35 [[Bloodbadge]] require 70 (1 stack plus 6) [[Cruxmotion]].
      - 1 [[Bloodbadge]] is kept.
      - An additional 34 [[Bloodbadge]] are required for the listed non-Rift-transferable crafting requirements.
        - [[Anti-Bite Scarf]] requires 2 [[Bloodbadge]] to upgrade to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity.
        - [[Leggings of the Coven]] requires [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]] requires 12 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Luscious Healing Melon]] requires 2 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear]] requires 8 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Silver-Twist Karambit]] requires 1 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Vampiric Timecharm]] requires 6 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - Crafting [[Warmer Wizard Face]] from [[Warm Wizard Face]] requires 2 [[Bloodbadge]].
    - [[Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake]] requires 8 [[Cruxmotion]].
    - Crafting additional copies of the Rift-transferable [[Vampire Dentist Relic]] and [[Weirder Tuba]] for both inside and outside the Rift without using the [[Auction House]] requires another 9 [[Bloodbadge]], or 18 [[Cruxmotion]], increasing the total requirement to 97 (1 stack plus 33) [[Cruxmotion]] and the overall total to [[Shadow Crux]] 151 (2 stacks plus 23) Shadow Crux.

## Obtaining
### Mob Drops

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv1]|[[Shy]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

## Usage
3 Shy Crux and 333 [[Motes]] can be used to purchase the [[Wyld Sword]] after speaking to all three [[Argofay Threebrother]].
6 Shy Crux can be used to purchase a [[Larva Hook]] from [[Argofay Bugshopper]].
2 Shy Crux and 400 [[Motes]] can be used to purchase an [[Empty Odonata Bottle]] from [[Argofay Bugshopper]] after purchasing a [[Larva Hook]].
Obtaining the [[Stability Elixir]] has a raw cost of 1 Shy Crux and 160 [[Motes]].
5 Shy Crux and 10,000 [[Motes]] can be used to purchase a [[Speed Elixir]] from [[Shifted]].
5 Shy Crux and 5,000 [[Motes]] can be used to purchase a [[Jump Elixir]] from [[Shifted]].

## Trivia
- A total of 175 Shy Crux are required for fixed one-time progression and the non-Rift-transferable requirements listed below, while keeping one [[Cruxmotion]] and one [[Bloodbadge]], assuming a separate Silkwire is created for the [[Argofay Bughunter]] quest and each of the four [[Enigma Souls]] listed below, and without accounting for additional [[Larva Silk]] from the [[Harmonious Surgery Toolkit]]:
  - [[Crux Talisman]] requires 8 Shy Crux.
  - [[Globulate Timecharm]] requires 20 Shy Crux.
  - [[Wyld Sword]] requires 3 Shy Crux.
  - A total of 41 [[Larva Silk]] are consumed by the listed one-time objectives. One [[Larva Hook]] can be used to obtain [[Larva Silk]] up to 8 times before disappearing. Without accounting for additional Silk from the [[Harmonious Surgery Toolkit]], obtaining all 41 requires 6 [[Larva Hook]], costing 36 Shy Crux.
    - 7 [[Larva Silk]] are given to the [[Argofay Bughunter]].
    - 2 [[Larva Silk]] are used to create the Silkwire required to capture an [[Odonata]] during the [[Argofay Bughunter]] quest.
    - 8 [[Larva Silk]] are used to create four Silkwires for the Between Branches, Between Roofs, Between Cooler Roofs, and Between Towers [[Enigma Souls]].
    - 16 [[Larva Silk]] are given to [[Reed]].
    - 8 [[Larva Silk]] are given to [[Alchemist (Rift)|Alchemist]] as part of the [[Detransfigured Seraphine]] quest.
  - A total of 14 [[Bottled Odonata]] are required for fixed one-time progression. Their 14 [[Empty Odonata Bottle]] require 28 Shy Crux.
    - 9 [[Bottled Odonata]] are required for the [[Argofay Bughunter]] quest.
    - [[Supreme Timecharm]] requires 4 [[Bottled Odonata]].
    - [[Harmonious Surgery Toolkit]] requires 1 [[Bottled Odonata]].
  - [[Stability Elixir]] requires 1 Shy Crux as part of its total acquisition cost.
  - A total of 79 (1 stack plus 15) [[Cruxmotion]] are required, equivalent to 79 Shy Crux.
    - 1 [[Cruxmotion]] is kept.
    - A total of 35 [[Bloodbadge]] require 70 (1 stack plus 6) [[Cruxmotion]].
      - 1 [[Bloodbadge]] is kept.
      - An additional 34 [[Bloodbadge]] are required for the listed non-Rift-transferable crafting requirements.
        - [[Anti-Bite Scarf]] requires 2 [[Bloodbadge]] to upgrade to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity.
        - [[Leggings of the Coven]] requires 1 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]] requires 12 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Luscious Healing Melon]] requires 2 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear]] requires 8 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Silver-Twist Karambit]] requires 1 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - [[Vampiric Timecharm]] requires 6 [[Bloodbadge]].
        - Crafting [[Warmer Wizard Face]] from [[Warm Wizard Face]] requires 2 [[Bloodbadge]].
    - [[Super Sharp 'n Stabby Steak Stake]] requires 8 [[Cruxmotion]].
    - Crafting additional copies of the Rift-transferable [[Vampire Dentist Relic]] and [[Weirder Tuba]] for both inside and outside the Rift without using the [[Auction House]] requires another 9 [[Bloodbadge]], or 18 [[Cruxmotion]], increasing the total requirement to 97 (1 stack plus 33) [[Cruxmotion]] and the overall total to 193 Shy Crux.
- [[Speed Elixir]], [[Jump Elixir]], and [[Strength Elixir]] each require 5 Shy Crux to purchase, but they are repeatable consumables and are not included in the one-time progression total above.
- Additional [[Larva Silk]] used to create Silkwires beyond the [[Argofay Bughunter]] quest and the four listed [[Enigma Souls]] is not included in the total above.

## Trivia
- It is a common misconception that vampires are weak to silver. A Vampires weakness to silver is not found in traditional vampire folklore, but is instead derived from silver's relation to purity. Vampires are weakened by pure things such as crucifix's, rosary's, holy water and running water such as rivers.
- Cultural practices often arose that were intended to prevent a recently deceased loved one from turning into a vampire. One such practice was placing earthly objects, such as scythes, sickles, and other bladed objects.
  - The karambit was originally an agricultural tool designed to rake roots, gather threshing and plant rice originating in Indonesia, specifically on the island of Sumatra before spreading into neighbouring countries like Malaysia and the Philippines.

## Obtaining
### Crafting

Sword:
Silver-Twist Karambit [[Speed]]: +15 [[Rift Damage]]: +6
[Effects]
[Gain +5 Rift Damage against Vampires.; Refunds 6 Mana and 0.5 Hearts on hit.]
## Trivia
- It is a common misconception that vampires are weak to silver. A Vampires weakness to silver is not found in traditional vampire folklore, but is instead derived from silver's relation to purity. Vampires are weakened by pure things such as crucifixes, rosaries, holy water, and running water such as rivers.
- Cultural practices often arose that were intended to prevent a recently deceased loved one from turning into a vampire. One such practice was placing earthly objects, such as scythes, sickles, and other bladed objects.
  - The karambit was originally an agricultural tool designed to rake roots, gather threshing and plant rice originating in Indonesia, specifically on the island of Sumatra before spreading into neighbouring countries like Malaysia and the Philippines.

Tooltip: Place this timecharm in the Rift Gallery to secure it forever!; * Soulbound * SPECIAL RIFT TIMECHARM
The SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Rift Timecharms|Rift Timecharm]].
## Obtaining
The player can purchase a SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm from the [[Barry HQ|Old Election Box]] in [[Barry HQ]] for 16 [[Scribe Crux]] and 1 [[Barry's Montgray Pen]] after helping [[Barry]] with all the protestors.
## Usage
The player can secure the SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm in the [[Rift Gallery]] to unlock access to the [[Living Cave]] and thus the [[Living Stillness]].
### Game Message
Securing the SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm will result in the following message, which will be broadcast to the entire lobby:
RIFTASTIC! [player] has secured SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm into their gallery! gg!
### Rift Necklace
Securing the SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm will add additional [[Stats]] to the [[Rift Necklace]] both inside and outside the [[Rift Dimension]].

### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|4x [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] 4x [[Bottled Odonata]] 24x [[Deadgehog Spine]] 32x [[Lil' Pad]]|Recipe: [[Bottled Odonata]] ×4 + [[Lil' Pad]] x16 ×2 + [[Deadgehog Spine]] x12 ×2 + [[Leech Supreme Fragment]] x4 → Supreme Timecharm|

The player can bring a Supreme Timecharm to [[Elise (Rift)|Elise]] in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] to grant access to the [[Rift Gallery]], where they can secure the [[Rift Timecharms|Timecharm]]. Securing the Supreme Timecharm grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+100 SkyBlock XP]] and unlocks the [[West Village]] and thus all of the following locations:
- [[Dolphin Trainer]]
- [[Dreadfarm]]
- [[Great Beanstalk]]
- [[Infested House]]
- [[Mirrorverse]]

Securing the Supreme Timecharm will result in the following message, which will be broadcast to the entire lobby:

Item:

Tooltip: Place this timecharm in the Rift Gallery to secure it forever!; * Soulbound * SPECIAL RIFT TIMECHARM
The Vampiric Timecharm is a [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Rift Timecharms|Rift Timecharm]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|3x [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] 6x [[Bloodbadge]]|Recipe: [[Bloodbadge]] ×6 + [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] ×3 → Vampiric Timecharm|
## Usage
The player can secure the Vampiric Timecharm in the [[Rift Gallery]] to gain [[SkyBlock Levels|+100 SkyBlock XP]].
### Game Message
Securing the Vampiric Timecharm will result in the following message, which will be broadcast to the entire lobby:
RIFTASTIC! [player] has secured Vampiric Timecharm into their gallery! gg!
### Rift Necklace
Securing the Vampiric Timecharm will add additional [[Stats]] to the [[Rift Necklace]] both inside and outside the [[Rift Dimension]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Vampiric Timecharm.|
|May 18, 2023|Changed crafting recipe from 18 Bloodbadges ([[Bloodbadge]]) to 12 Bloodbadges ([[Bloodbadge]]).|
|Sep 16, 2024|Changed crafting recipe from 12 Bloodbadges ([[Bloodbadge]]) to 6 Bloodbadges ([[Bloodbadge]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Vampiric Timecharm.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Changed crafting recipe from 12 Bloodbadges ([[Bloodbadge]]) to 6 Bloodbadges ([[Bloodbadge]]).|

## Trivia
- The W Logo, and by extension [[W Logo Fragment]], is likely a reference to Superman's logo.

  - The items are still referred to with S in their IDs.
- The W Logo shares the Color ⁠ #FFD700 with [[Haymaker Armor|Farm Armor]] and [[Eleanor's Armor]].

Wilted Berberis is a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] [[Collections|Collection]] Item.
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|20|[[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] Recipe|+4|
|II|60|[[Berberis Blowgun]] Recipe|+4|
|III|140|[[Jinxed Voodoo Doll]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|400|[[Berberis Fuel Injector]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Wilted Berberis can be harvested with a [[Wand of Farming]] in the [[Dreadfarm]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. The player must collect the Dead Bushes with purple particles in order to obtain Wilted Berberis. After harvesting a Wilted Berberis plant, the particles will move to another one in the area.
## Usage
### Reed's Quest
The player can give [[Wilted Berberis]] 64 (1 stack) Wilted Berberis to [[Reed]] will complete Step #2 and grant the player a permanent +20 [[Rift Time]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Berberis Blowgun]]|Wilted Berberis II (2)|[[Wilted Berberis]] 60x Wilted Berberis|Recipe: Wilted Berberis x20 ×3 → [[Berberis Blowgun]]|
|[[Berberis Fuel Injector]]|Wilted Berberis IV (4)|[[Wilted Berberis]] 336x (5 stacks plus 16 (336 in total)) Wilted Berberis|Recipe: Wilted Berberis x48 ×7 → [[Berberis Fuel Injector]]|
|[[Wilted Berberis Bunch]]|Wilted Berberis I (1)|[[Wilted Berberis]] 20x Wilted Berberis|Recipe: Wilted Berberis x4 ×7 → [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]]|
|[[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]]|None|1x [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]] 6x [[Nullified Metal]] [[Wilted Berberis]] 24x Wilted Berberis|Recipe: [[Nullified Metal]] x2 ×3 + Wilted Berberis x24 + [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]] → [[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]]|
|[[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]]|None|1x [[Leech Sword]] 6x [[Nullified Metal]] [[Wilted Berberis]] 24x Wilted Berberis|Recipe: [[Nullified Metal]] x2 ×3 + Wilted Berberis x24 + [[Leech Sword]] → [[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]]|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WILTED_BERBERIS_BUNCH
Tooltip: Collection Item; The plant equivalent of... ALIEN revenant viscera?; X Rift-Transferable X UNCOMMON
The Wilted Berberis Bunch is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wilted Berberis I (1) ([[Wilted Berberis]])|20x [[Wilted Berberis]]|Recipe: [[Wilted Berberis]] x4 ×7 → Wilted Berberis Bunch|
## Usage
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Wilted Berberis Bunch can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. Once in the [[Hub Island|Hub]], Wilted Berberis Bunch can be used to craft the [[Jinxed Voodoo Doll]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]]|None|1x [[Orange Chestplate]] 12x [[Bloodbadge]] [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] 24x Wilted Berberis Bunch 64x [[Living Metal Heart]]|Recipe: [[Living Metal Heart]] x32 ×2 + Wilted Berberis Bunch x6 ×4 + [[Orange Chestplate]] + [[Bloodbadge]] x12 → [[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]]|
|[[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]]|None|1x [[Proto-Chicken]] 1x [[Agaricus Cap Bunch]] 1x [[Caducous Stem Bunch]] [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] 2x Wilted Berberis Bunch 8x [[Metaphoric Egg]]|Recipe: [[Caducous Stem Bunch]] + [[Metaphoric Egg]] x2 ×4 + [[Agaricus Cap Bunch]] + [[Proto-Chicken]] + Wilted Berberis Bunch ×2 → [[Chicken N Egg Timecharm]]|
|[[Vampiric Timecharm]]|None|[[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] 3x Wilted Berberis Bunch 6x [[Bloodbadge]]|Recipe: [[Bloodbadge]] ×6 + Wilted Berberis Bunch ×3 → [[Vampiric Timecharm]]|
|[[Steak Stake]]|Vampire Slayer II (2) ([[Vampire Slayer]])|[[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] 3x Wilted Berberis Bunch 18x [[Coven Seal]]|Recipe: [[Coven Seal]] x3 ×6 + Wilted Berberis Bunch ×3 → [[Steak Stake]]|
|[[Jinxed Voodoo Doll]]|Wilted Berberis III (3) ([[Wilted Berberis]])|1x [[Voodoo Doll]] 1x Voidling Minion II ([[Voidling Minion]]) 1x [[Zombie's Heart]] 3x [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] 13x [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] [[Wilted Berberis Bunch]] 64x Wilted Berberis Bunch|Recipe: Wilted Berberis Bunch x16 ×4 + [[Enchanted Pufferfish]] x3 + [[Voidling Minion]] + [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] x13 + [[Zombie's Heart]] + [[Voodoo Doll]] → [[Jinxed Voodoo Doll]]|

Wizardman Leggings are [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Leggings]] in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]].
## Obtaining

## Usage
Leggings:
Wizardman Leggings [[Rift Time]]: +100 [[Mana Regen]]: +8 [[Hearts]]: +1
[Effects]
[Ability: Friendship is Power; Gain +1 Rift Damage and +2 Mana Regen for each other wearer within 65 blocks.; Max of 3 players!; Same color = 2x stats!]
### Interface
The player can left-click with Wizardman Leggings to open an interface, allowing them to select the color of the leggings.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Changing color|Updated your pants color! It's like the Hypixel Pit!|
## Tips
- It is recommended to use Black color to maximize the stat buffs from the Wizardman Leggings, as it is what the majority of players have informally agreed to use.

Wyld Armor is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Armor]] set that can be bought with [[Motes]] from various [[Argofay Trafficker|Argofay Traffickers]] in the [[Wyld Woods]].
## Obtaining
Each Wyld Armor piece can be obtained from 4 different [[Argofay Trafficker|Argofay Traffickers]] for [[Motes]].
- [[Wyld Armor|Wyld Helmet on a Helmet]]: Argofay Trafficker at [[Coordinates|-150, 112, 114]] (1,000 [[Motes]])
- [[Wyld Armor|Wyld Chain Top]]: Argofay Trafficker at [[Coordinates|-137, 97, 112]] (2,000 [[Motes]])
- [[Wyld Armor|Wyld Leggings]]: Argofay Trafficker at [[Coordinates|-91, 90, 106]] (1,000 [[Motes]])
- [[Wyld Armor|Wyld Boots]]: Argofay Trafficker at [[Coordinates|-136, 88, 114]] (50 [[Motes]])
## Usage

|-|-|
|Wyld Helmet on a Helmet ([[Wyld Armor]]); [[Intelligence]]: +10 [[Rift Time]]: +15 Helmets All The Way Down; Regain +1 R.Tme ([[Rift Time]]) when killing a monster.; Note: Monsters already grant time on death.|Wyld Chain Top ([[Wyld Armor]]); [[Rift Time]]: +15|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Wyld Leggings ([[Wyld Armor]]); [[Rift Time]]: +15|Wyld Boots ([[Wyld Armor]]); [[Rift Time]]: +15|
[Total]
[Wyld Armor Intelligence: +10 Rift Time: +60]
### [[EtalptsehC norI]]
An [[EtalptsehC norI]] can be purchased from [[Dr. Emmett]] for 1 [[Wyld Armor|Wyld Chain Top]] and 12 [[Shadow Crux|Shadow Cruxes]] after completing the [[Mirrorverse]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Not-Dead-Gehog Mask]]|None|1x Wyld Helmet on a Helmet ([[Wyld Armor]]) 10x [[Deadgehog Spine]]|Recipe: [[Deadgehog Spine]] x2 ×5 + [[Wyld Armor]] → [[Not-Dead-Gehog Mask]]|
|[[Chicken Leggs]]|None|1x Wyld Leggings ([[Wyld Armor]]) 16x [[Volt Crux]] 32x [[Metaphoric Egg]]|Recipe: [[Volt Crux]] x8 ×2 + [[Wyld Armor]] + [[Metaphoric Egg]] x8 ×4 → [[Chicken Leggs]]|
|[[Gunther's Sneakers]]|None|1x [[Gunthesizer Lichen]] 1x Wyld Boots ([[Wyld Armor]]) 8x [[Shadow Crux]]|Recipe: [[Shadow Crux]] x2 ×4 + [[Gunthesizer Lichen]] + [[Wyld Armor]] → [[Gunther's Sneakers]]|
## Trivia
- Wyld Armor is the only full armor set in the Rift.
- The ability Helmets All The Way Down is likely a reference to the philosophical saying, "Turtles all the way down".

# Midas Staff
Aliases: Midas' Staff, ⚚ Midas Staff,  Midas Staff
Categories: Scam-Prone Items, Legendary items, Temp/Using Skill Aliases, Weapons, Templates with deprecated argument names, Craftable items, Swords, Dark Auction

[Midas Staff]
Type: Weapon ([[Weapons]])
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Dungeon Level ([[Dungeoneering]]) Requirement: Catacombs XXV (25) ([[Catacombs]]); (When converted to Dungeon Item ([[Dungeon Items]]))
Stats: [[Damage]]: +130; [[Strength]]: +150; [[Intelligence]]: +50
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MIDAS_STAFF
Tooltip: Damage: +130; Strength: +150; Intelligence: +50; Ability: Molten Wave RIGHT CLICK Cast a wave of molten gold in the direction you are facing! Deals up to 12,000 damage.; Mana Cost: 500 Cooldown: 1s; Ability: Greed The ability damage bonus of this item is dependent on the price paid for it at the Dark Auction!; The maximum bonus of this item is 26,000 if the bid was 100,000,000; Coins or higher!; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Midas Staff is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]] that can be obtained from the [[Dark Auction]]. Its ability increases in power as more coins are spent on it by the player, similar to the [[Midas' Sword]], up to 100M [[Coins]].
## Obtaining
### Dark Auction
The Midas Staff can be obtained from the [[Dark Auction]] only when [[Scorpius]] is elected as mayor, costing a minimum of 100k [[Coins]] to purchase. It will always appear as the eighth and final item for sale.
## Usage
Sword:
Midas Staff [[Damage]]: +130 [[Strength]]: +150 [[Intelligence]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Molten Wave RIGHT CLICK; Cast a wave of molten gold in the direction you are facing! Deals up to 12,000 damage.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 500; Cooldown: 1s Ability: Greed; The ability damage bonus of this item is dependent on the price paid for it at the Dark Auction!; The maximum bonus of this item is 26,000 if the bid was 100,000,000 Coins or higher!]
Sword:
[[Midas Staff]] [[Damage]]: +130 [[Strength]]: +150 [[Intelligence]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Molten Wave RIGHT CLICK; Cast a wave of molten gold in the direction you are facing! Deals up to 6,000 - 40,000 damage.; Mana Cost (Intelligence): 500; Cooldown: 1s Ability: Greed; The ability damage bonus of this item is dependent on the price paid in the Dark Auction!; The maximum bonus of this item is 34,000 if the bid was 500,000,000 Coins or higher!]
Its ability casts a wave of gold blocks in front of the player, similar to the [[Tank]] class ability Seismic Wave. All enemies caught within the wave take the full ability damage. The wave has a length of 14 blocks and stops upon impact with walls and carpets. The ability has a 1-second cooldown and costs 500 [[Mana]] to use.
Its ability damage is increased as the coins spent on the Midas Staff increases, up to a maximum of 26,000 bonus base damage at 100M [[Coins]], or 32,000 ability damage in total. A [[Golden Dragon Pet]] also buffs the ability by up to 10%.
## Upgrading
The Midas Staff can be upgraded to the Midas Staff with 8 [[Golden Fragment|Golden Fragments]], allowing the price paid to be increased to 500,000,000 [[Coins]] paid.
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Midas Staff]] 1x Midas Staff 8x [[Golden Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Golden Fragment]] ×8 + Midas Staff → [[Midas Staff]]|
## Essence Crafting
This weapon can be upgraded using Gold Essence. Once converted, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XXV (25)]] to use while in Dungeons.
Essence Required; [[Gold Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|100|100|
|✪|50|150|
|✪✪|100|250|
|✪✪✪|150|400|
|✪✪✪✪|200; 10,000 [[Coins]]|600; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|250; 25,000 [[Coins]]|850; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The Molten Wave ability gold blocks do not visually appear on non-full blocks, notably carpets. This is a visual bug however, as it still deals damage as if the wave is rendered.
- The Molten Wave ability gold blocks blocks the [[Bonemerang]] projectiles.
  - It used to block other projectiles, most notably arrows. This makes the Midas Staff less likely to be used in higher floors as archers usually deal much more damage than mages. This was fixed in August 8 2023 Patch Notes.
- The Molten Wave ability gold blocks can get stuck within cobwebs.
- The wave cannot hit enemies too close to the player, about 1 block away.
- The Greed ability's bonus ability damage used to be bugged, which meant that the bonus ability damage was not boosted by [[Catacombs]] level in dungeons even if the staff was dungeonized. This was fixed in the 0.14 Bug Fixes 2 Update.
- This item is referred to as "Midas' Staff" in the Dark Auction, however the actual item name is "Midas Staff".
- The Midas Staff is also called the "Midas Spoon" or the "Spoon" by the community.
  - The name "Midas Spoon" possibly stems from the fact that the vanilla Midas Staff texture used to be a golden shovel.
- This weapon at 100,000,000 [[Coins]] paid has the second highest base magic damage of any weapon after [[Giant's Sword]].
- The ability is used by [[King Midas]] in Dungeons, who was released 3 months prior to this item.

# Midas' Sword
