Tooltip: Damage: +170; Gemstones: [] [⚔]; Ability: Greed The strength and damage bonus of this item is dependent on the price paid for it at the Dark Auction!; The maximum bonus of this item is 120; if the bid was 50,000,000 Coins or higher!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 20.; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Midas' Sword is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]] that can be obtained from the [[Dark Auction]]. It increases in power as more [[Coins]] are spent on it by the player, maxing at 50M [[Coins]] (or 250M [[Coins]] when upgraded).
## Obtaining
### Dark Auction
The Midas' Sword can be obtained from the [[Dark Auction]] round 1, 3 or 4, costing a minimum of 100k [[Coins]] to purchase.
## Usage
Sword:
Midas' Sword [[Damage]]: +170
[Effects]
[Ability: Greed; The strength and damage bonus of this; item is dependent on the price paid for it at the; Dark Auction! The maximum bonus of this item is 120 if the bid was 50,000,000 Coins or higher!; Price paid: 50,000,000 Coins; Damage Bonus: 120; Strength Bonus: 120]
Sword:
[[Midas' Sword]] [[Damage]]: +170
[Effects]
[Ability: Greed; The strength and damage bonus of this; item is dependent on the price paid for it at the; Dark Auction! The maximum bonus of this item is 225 if the bid was 250,000,000 Coins or higher!; Price paid: 250,000,000 Coins; Damage Bonus: 225; Strength Bonus: 225]
When purchased from the [[Dark Auction]], it gains a [[Damage]] and [[Strength]] bonus based on how much the winner paid for it. The maximum possible buff is +120 [[Damage]] and +120 [[Strength]] at 50,000,000 [[Coins]] paid. For the fragged version, it is +225 [[Damage]] and +225 [[Strength]] at 250,000,000 [[Coins]] paid.
The Greed ability can be deduced from the formula below, with each value being cumulative with the steps before it.
|Bid Amount; (coins)|[[Damage]] bonus formula; (x = Bid Amount)|Bonus at max|Cumulative|
|-|-|-|-|
|<1M|x50000|‭20‬|‭20‬|
|1M–2.5M|x−1000000100000|‭15‬|35|
|2.5M–7.5M|x−2500000200000|‭25‬|60|
|7.5M–25M|x−7500000500000|‭35‬|95|
|25M–50M|x−250000001000000|‭25‬|120|
|≥50M|120|120|120|
|Bid Amount; (coins)|[[Damage]] bonus formula; (x = Bid Amount)|Bonus at max|Cumulative|
|-|-|-|-|
|<1M|x50000|‭20‬|‭20‬|
|1M–2.5M|x−1000000100000|‭15‬|35|
|2.5M–7.5M|x−2500000200000|‭25‬|60|
|7.5M–25M|x−7500000500000|‭35‬|95|
|25M–100M|x−250000001000000|‭75‬|170|
|100M–130M|x−1000000001500000|‭20|190|
|130M–150M|x−1300000002000000|‭10|200|
|150M–250M|x−1500000004000000|‭25|225|
|≥250M|225|225|225|
The output is added to the base damage of 150 and the base strength of 0 to produce the stats of the sword, where the base price for the Midas' Sword is 100,000 [[Coins]].
A [[Golden Dragon Pet]] also buffs the ability by up to 10%.

## Upgrading
The Midas' Sword can be upgraded to the Midas' Sword with 8 [[Golden Fragment|Golden Fragments]], allowing the price paid to be increased to 250,000,000 [[Coins]].
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|[[Midas' Sword]] 1x Midas' Sword 8x [[Golden Fragment]]|Recipe: [[Golden Fragment]] ×8 + Midas' Sword → [[Midas' Sword]]|
## Essence Crafting
This weapon can be upgraded using Gold Essence. Once converted, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs XVII (17)]] to use while in [[Dungeons]].
Essence Required; [[Gold Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|Conversion (The cost of the conversion to a dungeon item.)|100|100|
|✪|25|125|
|✪✪|50|175|
|✪✪✪|75|250|

|✪✪✪✪✪|200; 25,000 [[Coins]]|575; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The minimum price required to gain the maximum stat buff without using [[Zarina]] is 50,040,000 [[Coins]], or 250,520,000 [[Coins]] for the fragged version.
  - It is possible to increase the price paid at [[Zarina]] after the fact, however this has an additional cost of 3 [[Stock of Stonks]].
- There are a few Midas' Swords with price paid values not possible through the [[Dark Auction]].
  - 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Mynetrix received a Midas' Sword with exactly 50,000,000 [[Coins]] coins paid.
  - 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] TimeDeo owns a Midas' Sword with 1 [[Coins|Coin]] paid, granted to him by an admin.
  - 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Pigicial owns a Midas' Sword with 49,999,999 [[Coins]] paid.
- The highest possible bid on a Midas' Sword is 2,146,000,000 [[Coins]], which is just 1,483,647 [[Coins]] shy of the 32-bit integer limit.
  - Due to the [[Dark Auction]] having fixed bid increments, the next bid option would be a negative amount of coins, which cannot be chosen because it is lower than the highest bid.
  - It is not possible to increase the price paid for a Midas' Sword beyond 250,000,000 [[Coins]] using [[Zarina]].
- This sword is a reference to King Midas, a figure from Greek mythology. He was granted one wish and to pick to turn everything he touched to gold. When he killed his daughter by turning her to gold, he realized he had been blinded by greed, which this sword's ability references.
  - The [[Midas Staff]] and [[King Midas]] miniboss also reference him.
- The [[Reforging|Gilded]] reforge can be applied to this sword using a [[Midas Jewel]], increasing its [[Damage]] and [[Strength]], and granting it a small chance to grant coins to nearby players upon killing mobs.
- In the past, when [[One For All]] buffed weapon base damage, it did not work on the bonus damage provided by the Greed ability.

# Abyssal Miner
Aliases: Zombie Miner
Categories: Sea Creatures, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Mobs

An Abyssal Miner breaks out of the water!
The Abyssal Miner is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up from water in the [[Mithril Deposits]], [[Precursor Remnants]], or [[Jungle]].
## Spawning
The Abyssal Miner can be fished up from water in the [[Mithril Deposits]], [[Precursor Remnants]], or [[Jungle]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXIV (24)]] to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Abyssal Miner is 90, resulting in a default catch chance of 2.05% (1 in 48.73 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
|[[Tracking]]|Varies|
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|An Abyssal Miner breaks out of the water!|

Abyssal Miner can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] to obtain [[Abyssal Miner Shard]] and +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.
### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.
